*Ishani Janveja,Davis Zhang,Seoyul Oh,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rift 系统，在卫星端视觉-语言模型推理中通过查询条件化的 tile 剪枝与弹性预填充来削减 token 预算，在 Jetson AGX Orin 上相比穷举分块推理将能耗降低 78%、延迟降低 69%，同时将准确率从 45% 提升至 73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载视觉-语言模型有望让卫星直接回答查询，但对高分辨率影像做穷举分块（tiled）推理速度慢且能耗高，严重制约其在能源受限的卫星平台上的实际部署。

Method: 作者识别出一种新现象——答案不变 token 冗余（AITR），即某些图像块（tiles）和视觉 token 可以移除而不影响最终答案。基于此提出 Rift，一个两阶段系统：先做查询条件化的 tile 剪枝，再进行弹性预填充（elastic prefill）以压缩 token 预算，并在 LLaVA-1.5 7B 上以 Jetson AGX Orin 为硬件平台评估。

Result: Rift 相比穷举分块推理将能耗降低 78%、延迟降低 69%，同时准确率从 45% 提升到 73%。

Conclusion: 利用答案不变 token 冗余进行查询条件化的 tile 剪枝与弹性预填充，可在显著降低能耗与延迟的同时提升准确率，为星载视觉-语言模型高效推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Onboard vision-language models could enable satellites to answer queries directly, but exhaustive tiled inference over high-resolution imagery is slow and energy-intensive. We identify answer-invariant token redundancy (AITR): image tiles and vision tokens that can be removed without changing the final answer. We present Rift, a two-stage system that performs query-conditioned tile pruning followed by elastic prefill to reduce token budget. We evaluate it on LLaVA-1.5 7B running on Jetson AGX Orin. Compared with exhaustive tiled inference, Rift reduces energy by 78% and latency by 69%, while increasing accuracy from 45% to 73%.

</details>


### [27] [Where Hallucinations Live: A Cross-Architecture Circuit in VQ-Tokenized Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29048)
*Shamanthak Hegde,Xiangrui Liu,Maitreya Patel,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对统一视觉语言模型中由向量量化(VQ)码本标记图像所引发的物体幻觉问题,通过激活修补识别出一个跨架构共享的早期层(layer 0)注意力路由回路,并提出三闸门诊断法区分携带与不携带该回路的模型,证明仅对L0层进行消融即可显著减少开放式生成中的物体幻觉,而非机制无关的解码方法所能达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉语言模型使用VQ码本对图像进行token化,在锚定是/否基准上惯常地产生物体幻觉,而现有解码期修复方法将其视为通用误校准,缺乏架构层面的解释,因此需要从架构和预训练角度重新审视物体幻觉的产生机制。

Method: 作者在跨越八个LLM家族的25个模型上使用激活修补技术,识别出VQ-token化VLM中共享的早期层(L0)注意力路由回路;提出三闸门诊断法区分携带与不携带该回路的模型,得到十个阳性模型(五个自然统一VQ-VLM及五个诱导变体)并排除其余十五个;通过单一变量架构替换(LLaVA-1.6 的 CLIP+MLP 替换为 VQ+Linear)成功安装该回路,而等计算量的MLP对照在相同数据上无法安装,从而将病态信号归因于向量量化本身;最后对比调优的VCD与DoLA基线。

Result: 诊断隔离出十个阳性模型(跨三个LLM家族的五个自然统一VQ-VLM及五个诱导变体),拒绝其余十五个;单一变量交换(CLIP+MLP → VQ+Linear)安装回路,匹配计算量的MLP对照无法复现,证明向量量化是病态信号来源,且路由路径由主干网络本身提供;调优DoLA在二值校准上获胜,但只有L0消融能降低开放式生成中的物体幻觉(CHAIR_i相对降低31%),而调优DoLA和VCD则保持不变或更差。

Conclusion: 统一VQ视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉应被重新理解为架构与预训练的性质,而非单纯的解码校准问题;针对L0层的消融提供了一种机制无关解码方法无法复现的定向干预手段。

Abstract: Unified vision-language models (VLMs) that tokenize images through a vector-quantized (VQ) codebook routinely hallucinate objects on grounded yes/no benchmarks, yet existing decoding-time fixes treat this as generic miscalibration without an architectural account. Using activation patching across twenty-five models spanning eight LLM families, we identify an early-layer ($L_0$) attention routing circuit shared across VQ-tokenized VLMs and propose a three-gate diagnostic that distinguishes the models carrying it from those that do not. The diagnostic isolates ten positive models (five natural unified-VQ VLMs across three LLM families and five induced variants) and rejects the remaining fifteen. A single-variable architectural swap (LLaVA-1.6 CLIP+MLP $\rightarrow$ VQ+Linear) installs the circuit, while a matched-compute MLP control on identical data does not, isolating vector quantization as the source of the pathological signal; the routing pathway that carries it is one that the backbone already provides. Against tuned VCD and DoLA baselines, tuned DoLA wins on binary calibration, but \textbf{only $L_0$ ablation reduces object hallucination in open-ended generation} (CHAIR$_i$ reduces by $31\,\%$ relatively, whereas tuned DoLA and VCD leave it unchanged or worsen it). These results recast object hallucination in unified VQ VLMs as a property of architecture and pretraining, and yield a targeted intervention that mechanism-agnostic decoding cannot replicate.

</details>


### [28] [EIB-Net: Entropy-Guided Information Bottleneck for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29064)
*Zhida Zhang,Xinlei Ma,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EIB-Net，一种基于熵引导的信息瓶颈网络，用于检测 AI 生成图像。核心思路是利用图像熵（IE）自动筛选低纹理（最低熵）图块，再通过变分信息瓶颈（VIB）学习紧凑且可泛化的特征。实验显示，仅用 2% 训练数据即可达 85.7% 准确率，并在跨生成器泛化上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逼真的 AI 生成图像（尤其是扩散模型）缺乏传统伪造图像中的局部伪造痕迹（local artifacts），现有方法多针对基于篡改的伪造。作者观察到生成模型优先保证全局语义、牺牲局部纹理保真度，因此低纹理区域是合成来源的关键判别线索，据此需要一种能自动聚焦低纹理区域并学习可泛化特征的检测方法。

Method: 提出 EIB-Net（Entropy-guided Information Bottleneck Network）：(1) 设计图像熵 IE（Image Entropy）指标，自动选择信息量最大（熵最低）的图块；(2) 采用变分信息瓶颈 VIB（Variational Information Bottleneck）对所选图块进行处理，学习紧凑且可泛化的特征；(3) 熵引导的图块选择模块 EGPL（Entropy-Guided Patch Selection）可灵活嵌入 CNN 与 Transformer 等不同骨干网络。

Result: 在 DIFF、DiffusionForensics 和 GenImage 三个基准上取得最先进性能：仅用 2% 训练数据即达 85.7% 准确率，比全图基线高出 15% 以上；在 GenImage 上跨生成器平均准确率为 83.5%，保持稳健泛化；EGPL 能一致提升多种 CNN 和 Transformer 骨干网络的表现。

Conclusion: EIB-Net 通过熵引导聚焦低纹理区域并结合变分信息瓶颈，有效解决生成图像的检测与跨生成器泛化问题，在数据高效场景下显著超越全图基线，且 EGPL 模块具有通用性，证明了该方法在数据高效检测中的实用价值。

Abstract: The proliferation of photorealistic AI-generated images demands robust detection methods that generalize across diverse generative models. While existing approaches target manipulation-based forgeries with local artifacts, generation-based images (e.g., from diffusion models) lack such traces, posing a fundamental challenge. We observe that generative models prioritize global semantics at the expense of local texture fidelity, making low-texture regions key indicators of synthetic origin. To exploit this, we propose EIB-Net, an Entropy-guided Information Bottleneck Network. EIB-Net introduces a novel Image Entropy (IE) metric to automatically select the most informative (lowest-entropy) patch, then processes it with a Variational Information Bottleneck (VIB) to learn compact, generalizable features. Extensive experiments on DIFF, DiffusionForensics, and GenImage benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: EIB-Net achieves 85.7\% accuracy using only 2\% of training data, outperforming full-image baselines by over 15\%, and maintains robust cross-generator generalization (83.5\% average accuracy on GenImage). Furthermore, our entropy-guided patch selection (EGPL) consistently enhances diverse backbones (CNNs and Transformers), proving its practical value for data-efficient detection.

</details>


### [29] [Seeing Is Not Measuring: Tool-Augmented Metric Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29073)
*Kai Glantz,Clemens Grange*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个模块化、与预测器无关的工具增强框架，让小型VLM（Qwen3.5-4B）借助3D目标检测、度量深度估计以及距离/尺寸/方位求解器，将度量3D推理从模型权重转移到显式求解器，在ReVSI-Bench四项任务中的三项上取得大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）擅长场景描述，但对绝对距离、物理尺寸、以自我为中心的方向等度量3D结构推理能力差。作者希望将度量计算移出模型权重，交给显式、确定性的几何工具，以分离感知误差与推理误差并显著提升3D推理性能。

Method: 提出模块化、预测器无关、工具增强的框架：为小型VLM（Qwen3.5-4B）配备几何工具，包括3D目标检测、度量深度估计，以及距离、尺寸、方位的确定性求解器。每个物体在其最佳视角的相机坐标系中被检测，工具利用该帧位姿把所有检测结果提升（lift）到统一的共享世界坐标系。由于工具接口后可替换任意检测器，可用真实检测器与真值框对比来分离感知误差和推理误差。

Result: 在ReVSI-Bench四项任务中的三项取得显著提升：使用强单目检测器（WildDet3D）时，绝对距离平均相对精度（MRA）从0.46升至0.74，相对距离从39.1%升至67.4%，相对方向从低于随机水平的25.9%升至73.4%。编排（orchestration）本身仅损失0.03 MRA。物体尺寸受检测器限制：工具在真值框上几乎精确（0.97），但最佳真实检测器仅略高于无工具基线（0.61对0.58），因为尺寸直接读取单目检测器会搞错的框尺寸。无需预定义流程时，模型已能自行正确排序工具，在三项任务上与脚本化流水线持平。

Conclusion: 把度量计算外包给显式几何工具能大幅提升VLM的度量3D推理能力，且框架预测器无关、可替换检测器以分离感知与推理误差；框架的编排开销极小，但整体性能仍受检测器（尤其物体尺寸）的感知质量限制。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) describe scenes well but reason poorly about metric 3D structure such as absolute distances, physical sizes, or egocentric directions. We present a modular, predictor agnostic, tool-augmented framework that equips a small VLM (Qwen3.5-4B) with geometric tools: 3D object detection, metric depth estimation, and deterministic solvers for distance, size and bearing. Each object is detected in the camera frame of its own best view, and the tools use that frame's pose to lift every detection into one shared world frame. Moving metric computation out of the model's weights and into explicit solvers yields large gains on three of four ReVSI-Bench tasks: with a strong monocular detector (WildDet3D), absolute distance rises from 0.46 to 0.74 Mean Relative Accuracy (MRA), relative distance from 39.1% to 67.4%, and relative direction from a below-chance 25.9% to 73.4%. Because any detector can be swapped in behind the tool interface, comparing real detectors against ground-truth boxes separates perception error from reasoning error: orchestration costs only 0.03 MRA. Object size is bounded by the detector: the tools are near-exact on groundtruth boxes (0.97) yet the best real detector barely beats the no-tool baseline (0.61 vs. 0.58), because size reads straight off a box extent monocular detectors get wrong. Without a predefined recipe, the model already sequences the tools correctly on its own, matching a scripted pipeline on three of four tasks.

</details>


### [30] [WildHSR: Metric Feed-Forward 4D People-Scene Reconstruction from a 3D Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29106)
*Jerrin Bright,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildHSR 通过对一个仅到尺度(up-to-scale)的3D基础模型做轻量适配，同时恢复出度量尺度(metric scale)与持久的人物身份，从而实现单目视频中相机、场景和人物的度量级重建，并在多个基准上领先前馈方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型能一次前向恢复相机与几何，但最强模型往往缺乏尺度；而联合人-场景重建需要两个缺失输出——度量尺度与跨帧人物身份。度量级标签稀缺，但无标注的自然视频大量存在，因此作者希望检验：能否通过轻量适配，让一个仅到尺度的基础表征同时补上这两个能力。

Method: 提出双读出(dual readout)方案：(1) 尺度读出(Scale Readout)——利用网络视频中的人，以带位姿的度量人体和2D关键点得到近似闭式尺度伪标签来预训练，再用标准真实视频训练集上的精确度量监督对读出头和轻量适配器共同微调；推理时直接从基础模型token预测度量尺度。(2) 身份读出——探测预训练基础模型，发现其中间query-key特征编码了跨帧人物对应关系；用一个小投影读取该对应性，结合度量骨盆运动与提议置信度，驱动逐帧人体的 dustbin-aware Sinkhorn 关联。最终通过解析关联与 Sim(3) 组合连接各窗口，实现度量相机、场景与人物重建。

Result: 在EMDB-2上，WildHSR是公开对比中首个在前向方法里超越最佳基于优化的WA-MPJPE和RTE的方法，并在全部三个世界帧指标上领先前馈方法；在RICH上，它在WA-MPJPE和W-MPJPE上领先前馈的人-场景方法。完整流程在单GPU上达到10.1 fps。

Conclusion: 一个仅到尺度的3D基础表征可以通过轻量适配同时支撑度量尺度恢复与持久身份关联，无需相机参照物或教师网络，从而构建出首个能前向重建度量相机、场景和人物的单目视频系统。

Abstract: 3D foundation models recover video cameras and geometry in one forward pass, but some of the strongest are up to scale. Joint people-scene reconstruction then requires two missing outputs: metric scale and persistent person identity. We ask whether one up-to-scale foundation representation can support both through lightweight adaptation. Exact metric labels are scarce, but unlabeled in-the-wild video is abundant. We use people in curated web video to initialise the solution: a posed metric body and 2D keypoints give an approximate, closed-form scale pseudo-label. These pseudo-labels pretrain a Scale Readout, which is then fine-tuned together with a lightweight adapter using exact metric supervision from standard real-video training splits. At inference the head predicts metric scale from foundation-model tokens, without the ruler or its teachers. For person identity, we probe the pretrained foundation model alone and find evidence that its intermediate query-key features encode person correspondence across frames. In most evaluated moving-person clips, a mid-layer token prefers that person over the vacated location and other people. A tiny projection reads this correspondence; together with metric pelvis motion and proposal confidence, it drives dustbin-aware Sinkhorn association of per-frame bodies. WildHSR combines both readouts to reconstruct metric cameras, scene and people from monocular video. Each window is predicted feed-forward; analytic association and Sim(3) composition connect windows. On EMDB-2, WildHSR is the first feed-forward method in the published comparison to beat the best optimization-based WA-MPJPE and RTE while leading feed-forward methods on all three world-frame metrics. On RICH, it leads feed-forward people-and-scene methods on WA-MPJPE and W-MPJPE. The complete pipeline runs at 10.1 fps on one GPU.

</details>


### [31] [Spectral Amplitude Purification in Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29116)
*Zhenyu Zhou,Can Wang,Chun Chen,Zeyu Zheng,Defang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAP-DMD,一种即插即用的方法,通过自适应调制 DMD 方向场的幅度谱来抑制低频主导,加速蒸馏训练收敛并提升少步采样的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Distribution Matching Distillation(DMD)虽然能在少量步数内实现高质量扩散采样,但其优化动态被粗糙的低频信号主导,导致细粒度细节恢复缓慢。研究发现 DMD 方向误差的频谱幅度高度集中在低频,主导的低频分量压制了较弱的中高频信号。

Method: 提出 Spectral Amplitude Purification for Distribution Matching Distillation(SAP-DMD),一种即插即用方法,自适应调制 DMD 方向场的幅度谱;通过抑制幅度谱中占主导的尾部(低频分量)来降低低频主导性,从而促进细结构和纹理的有效恢复。

Result: 在 PixArt-α、SD3 和 SD3.5 上的实验表明,SAP-DMD 加速了训练收敛,并在 2 步和 4 步采样条件下均提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过频谱幅度纯化抑制低频主导、增强中高频信号,SAP-DMD 有效缓解了 DMD 优化中细节恢复迟缓的问题,以即插即用的方式提升了少步扩散蒸馏的效率与质量。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables high-quality diffusion sampling in only a few steps, but its optimization dynamics remain dominated by coarse, low-frequency signals, delaying the recovery of fine-grained details. We identify a pronounced concentration of spectral amplitudes at low frequencies in the DMD directional error, where dominant low-frequency components overwhelm weaker mid- and high-frequency signals. To address this issue, we propose Spectral Amplitude Purification for Distribution Matching Distillation (SAP-DMD), a plug-and-play approach that adaptively modulates the amplitude spectrum of the DMD directional field. By suppressing the dominant tail of the amplitude spectrum, SAP-DMD reduces low-frequency dominance and promotes more effective recovery of fine structures and textures. Experiments on PixArt-$α$, SD3, and SD3.5 demonstrate that SAP-DMD accelerates training convergence and improves generation quality under both 2-step and 4-step sampling.

</details>


### [32] [UpDown-SC: Gravity-Canonicalized Dual-Envelope Scan Context for Indoor LiDAR Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29118)
*Jie Xu,Yongxin Yang,Ziyi Jin,Kangjin Yu,Hongjun Huang,Chao Han,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UpDown-SC 是一种免训练、基于 Scan Context 改进的 LiDAR 地点识别描述子，通过将点云同时编码为“下/中层结构的上包络”与“顶部结构的下包络”两个互补表面，显著提升了室内及挂载高度变化场景下的检索鲁棒性，且保持轻量 CPU 前端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Scan Context 在每个极坐标单元中存储最大高度，室内场景中大面积天花板会抑制区分相邻房间与走廊的中低层几何信息；同时当建图与查询之间姿态或传感器挂载高度改变时，室内地点检索效果显著下降。因此需要一种能突出低层判别性结构、同时抑制高度变化影响的描述子。

Method: 提出 UpDown-SC：整体免训练。首先对重力方向做规范化（canonicalize gravity），然后表示两个互补表面——下方/中层结构的上包络（upper envelope）与头顶结构的下包络（lower envelope）。这两个表面的物理分界高度由一次性的、按单元平衡的地图高度分布估计得到，并被所有查询复用。距离度量采用掩码感知、非均匀的双通道距离，既保留低层判别性证据又限制其对跨会话变化的敏感度，且不把未观测单元当作零高度测量。保留传统 Scan Context 的候选短列表筛选与圆周偏航对齐，使检索得到的假设可直接初始化几何验证。

Result: 实验覆盖重复室内会话、挂载高度变化、室外到室内混合轨迹以及室外迁移序列。结果表明：在室内及挂载高度变化的会话中，UpDown-SC 的首选检索（first-choice retrieval）更可靠；配对检验显示其在自采集会话上相对 Scan Context 有显著提升。在基于阈值的接受判定下，UpDown-SC 取得最优或次优的 F1max 与 AUPR，同时保持轻量 CPU 前端。连续回放实验确认检索到的假设能够支撑度量级先验地图定位。

Conclusion: UpDown-SC 通过双表面编码与掩码感知的非均匀双通道距离，在不牺牲计算轻量性的前提下，有效解决了 Scan Context 在室内与挂载高度变化场景中的退化问题，并可直接为后续几何验证与先验地图定位提供可靠假设。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition is a key front end for loop closure and global relocalization, yet indoor retrieval remains difficult when attitude or sensor mounting height changes between mapping and query sessions. Scan Context stores the maximum height in each polar cell; indoors, broad ceilings can suppress the lower and mid-level geometry that distinguishes adjacent rooms and corridors. We present UpDown-SC, a training-free polar descriptor that first canonicalizes gravity and then represents two complementary surfaces: the upper envelope of lower/middle structures and the lower envelope of overhead structures. Their physical split is estimated once from a cell-balanced map height distribution and reused by every query. A mask-aware, non-uniform two-channel distance retains discriminative lower-level evidence while limiting sensitivity to its cross-session variation, without treating unobserved cells as zero-height measurements. Conventional Scan Context shortlisting and circular yaw alignment are retained, so retrieved hypotheses directly initialize geometric verification. Experiments across repeated indoor sessions, mounting-height changes, mixed outdoor-to-indoor trajectories, and an outdoor transfer sequence show more reliable first-choice retrieval on the indoor and mounting-height-varied sessions. A paired test finds a significant gain over Scan Context on the in-house sessions. UpDown-SC also gives the best or second-best F1max and AUPR under threshold-based acceptance while retaining a lightweight CPU front end. Continuous replay confirms that the retrieved hypotheses support metric prior-map localization. Code and evaluation artifacts: https://github.com/jiejie567/updown-sc.

</details>


### [33] [Less is More: Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29121)
*Ilpo Viertola,Vladimir Iashin,Sophie Tötterström,Esa Rahtu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅编码器的音视频语义分割模型 EASE，在保持与现有 SotA 相近甚至更优精度的同时，运行速度提升 3 倍、训练开销大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的 AVSS 方法大多沿袭图像分割模型的设计思想，而这些图像分割模型被证明含有对分割性能贡献甚微的冗余组件。因此，作者希望遵循这一洞察，简化 AVSS 模型结构，实现更快、更简单的音视频语义分割。

Method: 提出 Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation（EASE），采用仅编码器（Encoder-only）的架构设计，去除图像分割模型中冗余的解码器等组件，并联合音频与视觉信息进行语义分割。

Result: EASE 最高可达 365 FPS，比先前 SotA AVS 模型快约 3 倍，且精度相当；在不同骨干网络和输入分辨率下均取得 SotA AVSS 性能，训练仅需不到 11 GPU 小时。

Conclusion: 结果表明 AVSS 可以做到更简单、更快速，为未来研究和实时应用提供了可扩展的基础，并公开了代码、模型权重与样例。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (AVSS) aims to identify, segment, and classify sound-emitting objects in video frames. Previous Transformer-based AVSS approaches largely inherit design principles from image segmentation models. Recent studies show that these image segmentation models contain redundant components that contribute little to the segmentation performance. Following this insight, we propose Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation (EASE). EASE runs at up to 365 FPS, 3x faster than prior State-of-the-Art (SotA) AVS models at comparable accuracy, and trains in under 11 GPU-hours. Furthermore, we achieve SotA AVSS performance across different backbones and input resolutions. Our results demonstrate that AVSS can be both simpler and faster, providing a scalable foundation for future research and real-time applications. Code, model weights, and samples are available at https://ease-avs.notion.site

</details>


### [34] [FoCal: Frequency-Oriented Cross-Modal Interaction and Spectral Calibration for Aerial Visible-Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29125)
*Ben Liang,Chao Sui,Junqi Bai,Yuan Liu,Chunlai Li,Xiubao Sui,Qian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FoCal，一种面向航拍 RGB-IR 目标检测的频率导向框架。它通过频率感知双域校准（FADC）模块显式建模不同频率分量的跨模态交互，并通过差异引导频谱调制（DGSM）模块自适应增强、保留或衰减联合频谱，在 DroneVehicle、ESCVehicle 和 ATR-UMOD 上取得领先精度，且仅 3.0M 参数、113.6 FPS，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航拍 RGB-IR 多模态检测器主要关注空间域交互或单一频率特征增强，对不同频率分量的跨模态交互模式探索不足；同时光谱差异本身既包含有用的互补线索，也包含不可靠的模态特定响应，无差别的频率融合因此并非最优。

Method: 提出 FoCal 框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 频率感知双域校准模块（FADC），显式建模依赖频率的跨模态交互——低频分量被协同整合为共享结构共识，高频分量通过选择性跨模态交换保留模态特定信息，所得频率感知线索再传回原特征域调节跨模态校准；2) 差异引导频谱调制模块（DGSM），利用置信度加权的相对幅度差异刻画跨模态频谱不平衡，并将其转化为有界符号门控，对联合多模态频谱进行自适应增强、保留或衰减。

Result: 在 DroneVehicle、ESCVehicle 和 ATR-UMOD 上，FoCal 分别取得 83.5%、54.8% 和 64.6% 的 mAP50。同时仅用 3.0M 参数即达到 113.6 FPS，在保持领先检测精度的同时展现出良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: FoCal 通过频率感知的跨模态校准与差异引导的频谱调制，有效利用了 RGB 与 IR 模态间不同频率分量的互补信息，在三个航拍检测基准上取得先进性能，并以极低参数量和高速推理实现了优越的精度-效率平衡。

Abstract: In aerial RGB--IR object detection, effectively exploiting complementary information across modalities is critical for robust perception under complex illumination and environmental conditions. Existing multimodal detectors mainly focus on spatial-domain interaction or frequency-specific feature enhancement, while the cross-modal interaction patterns of different frequency components remain insufficiently explored. Moreover, spectral discrepancy itself may contain both useful complementary cues and unreliable modality-specific responses, making indiscriminate frequency fusion suboptimal. To address these issues, we propose FoCal, a frequency-oriented framework for aerial RGB--IR object detection. First, a Frequency-Aware Dual-Domain Calibration (FADC) module is developed to explicitly model frequency-dependent cross-modal interaction. Low-frequency components are collaboratively consolidated into a shared structural consensus, whereas high-frequency components preserve modality-specific information through selective cross-modal exchange. The resulting frequency-aware cues are further transferred to the original feature domain to regulate cross-modal calibration. Second, we introduce a Discrepancy-Guided Spectral Modulation (DGSM) module, which characterizes cross-modal spectral imbalance using confidence-weighted relative amplitude discrepancy and transforms it into a bounded signed gate for adaptive enhancement, preservation, or attenuation of the joint multimodal spectrum. Extensive experiments on DroneVehicle, ESCVehicle, and ATR-UMOD demonstrate the effectiveness of FoCal, yielding $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ values of 83.5\%, 54.8\%, and 64.6\%, respectively. Meanwhile, with only 3.0M parameters, FoCal achieves 113.6 FPS while preserving leading detection accuracy, highlighting a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off. Code is available at {https://github.com/universeliang/FoCal.

</details>


### [35] [Recoverable Geographic Location Information in Earth-Observation Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29151)
*Peiwen Zhang,Kristie Hu,Jovana Knezevic,Shunde Yin,Kyle Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估三个遥感基础模型（Tessera v1、v1.1、AlphaEarth）的嵌入是否包含可恢复的地理坐标信息，发现三者均存在可预测的地理信息，并提出将地理信息含量作为遥感基础模型审计的新标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下游任务精度无法反映基础模型嵌入是否编码了地理信息：地理信息对位置感知应用有益，但在需要位置不变表征时可能有害。因此需要专门评估嵌入的地理坐标鲁棒性。

Method: 使用2024年284个经质量验证的欧洲太阳能电站样本，测试能否从嵌入表征中预测坐标。通过余弦距离与测地距离的关联性，以及EPSG:3035投影坐标的预测任务来量化地理信息含量，并与训练范围内均匀随机采样基线及Sentinel-2对照进行比较。

Result: 三个遥感基础模型的嵌入均含可恢复的地理信息，所有预测模型均显著优于均匀随机采样基线；其中AlphaEarth距离关联最强、平均测地误差最低，两个Tessera版本的地理距离相关性也高于Sentinel-2对照。

Conclusion: 地理信息含量应作为审计遥感基础模型的额外准则，以帮助在不同应用场景（位置感知 vs. 位置不变）下选择和评估模型。

Abstract: Earth-observation (EO) foundation models provide reusable embeddings, yet downstream task accuracy does not reveal whether these representations encode geographic information, which may be beneficial for location-aware applications but potentially detrimental when representations invariant to geographic location are desired. We therefore evaluate the geographic coordinate robustness of Tessera v1, Tessera v1.1, and AlphaEarth by testing whether coordinates can be predicted from the embedding representations using 284 quality-verified European solar farms from 2024. We assessed geographic information content information through the association between cosine and geodesic distances and through prediction of projected coordinates in EPSG:3035. Embeddings from all three EO foundation models contain recoverable geographic information. All prediction models significantly outperform training-range uniform random sampling baselines, with AlphaEarth exhibiting the strongest distance association and lowest mean geodesic error. Both Tessera variants also yielded higher geographic distance correlations than the Sentinel-2 controls. These findings motivate geographic information content as an additional criterion for auditing EO foundation models.

</details>


### [36] [Med-AR: Autoregressive Vision-Language Pretraining for Long-Tailed Chest X-Ray Classification and Uncertainty-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29156)
*Janhavi Prabhu,Sahil,Akshay V,Shivam Shukla,Manoj Tadepalli,Preetham Putha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两个放射学原生自回归视觉-语言模型 Med-AR-8B 与 Med-AR-2B，其视觉编码器在长尾胸片多标签分类中，于 PadChest、MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert 上的多数患病率分组中超越了 Med-CLIP 等对比式、自监督与监督预训练编码器，并改善了选择性预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾胸部 X 光分类既需识别常见异常，也需捕捉罕见、细微的发现；现有视觉表征在此场景下对尾部类别判别力不足，需要更强的预训练方案。

Method: 提出 Med-AR-8B 与 Med-AR-2B 两个放射学原生自回归视觉-语言模型，用结构化报告、异常聚焦文本和区域标注进行预训练；将其视觉编码器迁移到多标签分类，并在统一的 ML-Decoder 分类头下，与 Med-CLIP、CheXFound、EVA-Base、ARK、BioViL-T 等编码器对比。同时用 LLM 扩展、从报告派生的标签集构建 MIMIC-CXR 与 CheXpert 的细粒度标签，评估判别（AUROC/AUPRC）与选择性预测（风险-覆盖曲线下超额面积）。

Result: Med-AR-8B 在 PadChest、MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert 的头、中、尾发现的平均 AUROC 与 AUPRC 上均超过 Med-CLIP；在 MIMIC-CXR 上将尾标签平均 AUPRC 从 0.1033 提升到 0.1441。Med-AR-2B 在 PadChest 取得最强判别结果。在全部三个公开数据集的所有汇报患病率分组上，均有某个 Med-AR 变体取得最高平均 AUROC/AUPRC；且两个 Med-AR 变体的风险-覆盖曲线下超额面积均低于 Med-CLIP，选择性预测更优。内部结果则依指标而异，Med-CLIP 在整体及尾部 AUPRC 和选择性预测上仍占优。

Conclusion: Med-AR 是在所评估公开基准上进行长尾胸片分类的强预训练方案，并证明将判别性能与选择性预测联合评估的价值。

Abstract: Long-tailed chest X-ray classification requires visual representations that capture both common abnormalities and subtle, infrequent findings. We propose Med-AR-8B and Med-AR-2B, two radiology-native autoregressive vision-language models pretrained with structured reports, abnormality-focused text, and region annotations. We evaluate the transfer of their visual encoders to multi-label classification against contrastive, self-supervised, and supervised pretrained encoders, including Med-CLIP, CheXFound, EVA-Base, ARK, and BioViL-T, using a common ML-Decoder classification head. To assess fine-grained recognition, we also construct LLM-expanded, report-derived label sets for MIMIC-CXR and CheXpert. Across PadChest, MIMIC-CXR, and CheXpert, Med-AR-8B outperforms Med-CLIP in mean AUROC and AUPRC for head, medium, and tail findings. On MIMIC-CXR, it increases tail-label mean AUPRC from 0.1033 to 0.1441. Med-AR-2B achieves the strongest discrimination results on PadChest. Across the broader encoder comparison, a Med-AR variant achieves the highest mean AUROC and AUPRC in every reported prevalence group on each public dataset. Both Med-AR variants also achieve lower excess area under the risk-coverage curve than Med-CLIP on all three public datasets, indicating improved selective-prediction performance under the evaluated protocol. Internal results are metric-dependent, with Med-CLIP retaining advantages in overall and tail AUPRC and in selective prediction. These findings establish Med-AR as a strong pretraining recipe for long-tailed chest X-ray classification on the evaluated public benchmarks and demonstrate the value of assessing discrimination and selective prediction together.

</details>

*Dibyayan Patra,Simit Raval,Pasindu Ranasinghe,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-TARGeT框架，利用低成本、通用、非唯一矩形标签，实现地下矿山采场点云与矿山参考坐标系的自动化配准与地理参考，达到厘米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出3D-TARGeT（3D Tag-based Automated Registration and Georeferencing Technique）自动化框架，通过低成本、通用、非唯一的矩形标签在采场点云与矿山参考坐标系间建立空间对应关系。框架整合了自动化标签识别、几何标签匹配与刚体变换估计三个环节。

Result: 以某地下矿山采场的四个多时相点云扫描为概念验证，在代表性扫描条件下模拟标签。实验显示3D-TARGeT实现了稳定厘米级地理参考精度，所有扫描的点云间中位距离与均方根误差均低于0.03 m，并显著优于广泛使用的自动点云配准技术。

Conclusion: 3D-TARGeT为采场点云地理参考提供了一种准确且稳健的自动化方案，减少了对人工对齐的依赖，有助于构建多时相数据集，支持下游地质与岩土工程应用。

Abstract: The increasing use of UAV laser scanning in underground mines has enabled frequent acquisition of 3D point clouds from challenging environments such as stopes, generating large volumes of multi-temporal spatial data throughout successive excavation stages. However, in GNSS-denied underground environments, independently acquired stope point clouds are generated within local scanner reference frames and require registration and georeferencing before integration with mine reference data for downstream geotechnical analysis, monitoring, and mine planning. This process is commonly performed manually by aligning individual stope scans with mine reference drives, making repeated georeferencing time-consuming and potentially limiting the utilisation of routinely acquired data. This study proposes the 3D Tag-based Automated Registration and Georeferencing Technique (3D-TARGeT), an automated framework using low-cost, generic, non-unique rectangular tags to establish spatial correspondence between stope point clouds and the mine reference coordinate system. The framework combines automated tag identification, geometric tag matching, and rigid transformation estimation. It was evaluated as a proof of concept using four multi-temporal point-cloud scans of an underground mine stope, with the proposed tags simulated under representative scanning conditions. 3D-TARGeT achieved consistent centimetre-level georeferencing accuracy, with median cloud-to-cloud distance and root mean square error below 0.03 m across all scans, while substantially outperforming widely used automatic point-cloud registration techniques. Overall, 3D-TARGeT provides an accurate and robust approach for automating stope point-cloud georeferencing, reducing reliance on manual alignment and facilitating multi-temporal datasets for downstream geological and geotechnical applications.

</details>


### [38] [ImCorr: Sub-pixel Semantic Correspondence via Implicit Feature Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29193)
*Yusung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ImCorr，通过隐式特征解码将语义对应估计从离散网格提升为连续特征场上的查询，从而消除表示层的量化误差，在细粒度阈值（PCK@0.01–0.05）上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语义对应方法在标准阈值下表现强劲，但在细粒度阈值处性能急剧停滞。作者认为这并非骨干特征表示能力不足，而是源于网格化读出的量化误差：patch 视觉Transformer将图像离散化为网格，源侧只能用最近邻 patch 特征近似精确关键点，目标侧则缺少表示精确真值位置的网格特征。该结构限制与匹配策略无关，是表示层的根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出 ImCorr（Sub-pixel Semantic Correspondence via Implicit Feature Decoding）：将对应估计构建在可在任意连续坐标查询的连续特征场上。使用 FiLM 条件解码器将亚像素位置信息嵌入特征场；直接在精确关键点坐标查询特征场，从理论上消除源侧表示层量化误差；同时在比骨干网格更密的网格上解码，大幅降低目标侧量化误差。

Result: 在 SPair-71k 和 AP-10K（种内、跨种、跨科）上，ImCorr 在细粒度阈值（PCK@0.01–0.05）上提升性能，在 SPair-71k 的 PCK@0.01 上较先前最优方法提升 6.2 个百分点。量化分析显示，在标准 448×448、patch-14 设置下，SPair-71k 全部 499,188 个关键点中 84.9% 的真值关键点在 PCK@0.01 下缺乏表示其精确位置的网格特征。

Conclusion: 表示层连续性是实现精确语义对应的有效方案，打破网格量化瓶颈能够显著改善细粒度阈值下的对应精度。

Abstract: The strong performance that modern semantic correspondence methods achieve at standard thresholds plateaus sharply at fine-grained thresholds. We argue that this plateau stems not from the representational capacity of backbone features, but from a grid-tied readout. Patch-based vision transformers tokenize images onto discrete grids, introducing two forms of quantization error: querying nearest patch features instead of exact keypoints on the source side, and the absence of grid features representing precise ground-truth locations on the target side. We quantify this quantization ceiling across all 499,188 keypoints in SPair-71k: under the standard 448x448, patch-14 setting, 84.9% of ground-truth keypoints have no grid feature representing their precise location at PCK@0.01. This is a structural limitation at the representation level, independent of the matching strategy. We address this with ImCorr: Sub-pixel Semantic Correspondence via Implicit Feature Decoding, which formulates correspondence estimation over a continuous feature field queryable at arbitrary continuous coordinates. A FiLM-conditioned decoder is trained to embed sub-pixel positional information into the feature field. Querying the field directly at exact keypoint coordinates theoretically eliminates representation-level quantization error on the source side, while decoding onto a grid denser than the backbone grid substantially reduces quantization error on the target side. On SPair-71k and AP-10K (intra-species, cross-species, and cross-family), ImCorr improves performance at fine-grained thresholds (PCK@0.01-0.05), achieving a 6.2 percentage point gain over the prior state of the art at PCK@0.01 on SPair-71k. These results demonstrate that representational continuity is an effective solution for precise semantic correspondence. Code is available at https://github.com/YusungChoi/ImCorr.

</details>


### [39] [FounRef: Robust, Structure-Preserving, and Fast Metric Refinement of Frozen Monocular Foundation Priors with Sparse Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29224)
*Dan Halperin,Mirko Mählisch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FounRef 是一种免训练方法，通过将冻结的单目基础模型深度先验与稀疏度量锚点对齐来生成稠密度量深度，兼具高精度、几何保真和快速推理，且各模块可独立替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密度量深度对真实世界 3D 应用至关重要，但现有方法难以同时满足精度、几何保真和推理速度。单目基础模型提供丰富的可迁移几何先验却缺乏可靠度量尺度；深度补全网络能恢复度量深度，但牺牲了几何保真度、跨域鲁棒性或速度。因此需要在度量对齐与几何预测之间解耦，兼顾三者。

Method: 提出 FounRef，一种免训练的模块化方法：深度先验、锚点来源和精化求解器均可独立替换。实例化采用 MoGe-2 作为深度先验、LiDAR 作为度量锚点。首先用先验的稠密深度预测验证每个锚点，剔除由跨传感器失配引起、纯几何滤波器无法检测的不一致锚点；随后通过结构保持求解器施加全局与局部度量校正，保留先验的细粒度几何。

Result: 在域外数据上，与最先进的深度补全网络 DMD3C 相比，FounRef 深度误差降低最多 24%，表面法线噪声降低 92%，推理速度提升近 15 倍。无需任务特定训练，可开箱即用地用于陌生相机和场景。

Conclusion: FounRef 通过将度量对齐与几何预测解耦，实现了准确、几何保真且高效的稠密度量深度估计，并能直接受益于未来基础模型和度量传感器的进步。

Abstract: Dense metric depth from cameras is essential to real-world 3D applications, yet achieving accuracy, faithful surface geometry, and fast inference simultaneously remains challenging. Monocular foundation models provide rich, transferable geometric priors but lack reliable metric scale, while depth-completion networks recover metric depth at the cost of geometric fidelity, cross-domain robustness, or speed. We present FounRef, a training-free method that aligns a frozen monocular foundation prior with sparse metric anchors to produce dense metric depth. FounRef is modular by design: its depth prior, anchor source, and refinement solver can each be replaced independently. We instantiate FounRef with MoGe-2 and LiDAR anchors. FounRef validates each anchor against the prior's dense depth prediction, rejecting inconsistencies caused by cross-sensor misalignment that geometry-only filters cannot detect. It then applies global and local metric corrections through a structure-preserving solver, retaining the prior's fine-grained geometry. FounRef requires no task-specific training and operates out of the box across unfamiliar cameras and scenes. On out-of-domain data, it delivers up to 24% lower depth error, 92% lower surface-normal noise, and almost 15x faster inference than DMD3C, a state-of-the-art depth-completion network. By decoupling metric alignment from geometry prediction, FounRef provides an accurate, geometrically faithful, and efficient approach to dense metric depth that can directly benefit from future advances in foundation models and metric sensors.

</details>


### [40] [ComplexSync: High-Fidelity and Real-Time Lip Sync in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29225)
*Jiaran Cai,Xingpei Ma,Shenneng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ComplexSync 是一个基于扩散模型的统一口型同步框架，通过双流联合训练、蒸馏单步去噪加速和基于视觉基础模型结构先验的关系对齐损失，在复杂场景下实现高保真、实时（>70 FPS）的口型同步，并发布了首个复杂口型同步基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在口型同步任务中虽生成质量高，但在复杂场景下表现不佳，且推理延迟过高，难以实际部署；同时缺乏针对复杂条件的专用基准来评估此类方法。

Method: 提出 ComplexSync：1）双流联合训练策略，缓解参考帧信息泄漏并保留自然动态；2）基于蒸馏的加速方案实现单步去噪，吞吐量超 70 FPS；3）关系对齐损失，利用视觉基础模型（VFM）的结构先验增强对复杂场景因素的鲁棒性；4）构建首个复杂口型同步基准，含 200+ 段挑战性视频和专用评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明，ComplexSync 在标准场景和复杂场景下均取得 state-of-the-art 性能，同时支持实时推理（单步去噪，>70 FPS），验证了各项设计（双流训练、蒸馏加速、关系对齐损失）的有效性。

Conclusion: ComplexSync 首次在扩散模型框架下同时解决口型同步的质量、鲁棒性与实时性问题，并通过发布复杂场景基准推动了该领域的可复现评估，展现出在真实世界部署的潜力。

Abstract: Lip synchronization aims to generate visual lip dynamics that align precisely with speech audio. Despite the high generation quality of diffusion models, they often struggle in complex scenarios and suffer from prohibitive inference latency, limiting real-world deployment. We present ComplexSync, a unified diffusion-based framework that enables real-time, high-fidelity lip sync under complex conditions. First, we introduce a dual-stream joint training strategy to mitigate information leakage from reference frames while preserving natural dynamics. Second, we develop a distillation-based acceleration scheme for single-step denoising, achieving a throughput of over 70 FPS. Third, we propose a relational alignment loss that leverages structural priors from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to enhance robustness against complex scene factors. Furthermore, we present the first benchmark specifically designed for complex lip synchronization, comprising over 200 challenging video sequences and specialized metrics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ComplexSync achieves state-of-the-art performance across both standard and complex scenarios while enabling real-time inference.

</details>


### [41] [SARFusion: Scene-Aware Routing Fusion for Robust Camera-LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29235)
*Yuting Zhao,Ziyi Zheng,Shuxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SARFusion 将稳健的相机-LiDAR融合建模为场景感知的分支路由问题，通过三个并行推理分支（相机、LiDAR、融合）和查询级路由策略，缓解跨模态干扰，在nuScenes上达到72.5 mAP和74.4 NDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合检测器通过紧耦合的多模态表示解码目标查询，在恶劣驾驶条件下对不可靠模态脆弱，退化的观测会干扰可靠的模态证据。此外，模态可靠性在全局场景和个体查询间均有差异，需要更细粒度的自适应融合决策。

Method: 提出SARFusion，将目标查询解码解耦为三个并行的推理分支：相机分支、LiDAR分支和相机-LiDAR融合分支。该方法根据全局驾驶上下文估计场景可靠性先验（Scene Reliability Prior），并结合目标级证据，对每个查询路由到最合适的分支，实现查询级路由策略。

Result: 在nuScenes测试集上，SARFusion取得72.5 mAP和74.4 NDS的强性能。在传感器损坏和环境变化等挑战性条件下，大量分析证明了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过查询级分支路由，SARFusion在减轻有害跨模态干扰的同时，保留了当互补线索可信时多模态融合的优势，提升了3D目标检测的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR fusion has become a prevailing paradigm for 3D object detection in autonomous driving. However, existing fusion detectors often establish strong inter-modality dependencies by decoding object queries from tightly coupled multimodal representations. Under corrupted driving conditions, such dependencies make the detector vulnerable to unreliable modalities, where degraded observations may interfere with reliable modality-specific evidence and lead to suboptimal predictions. Moreover, modality reliability can vary across both global driving scenes and individual object queries, requiring adaptive fusion decisions at a finer granularity. To bridge this gap, we reformulate robust camera-LiDAR fusion as a scene-aware branch routing problem and propose SARFusion, a robust 3D object detector. Instead of producing detections from a single fused representation, SARFusion decouples object-query decoding into three parallel reasoning branches: a camera branch, a LiDAR branch, and a camera-LiDAR fusion branch. Guided by a Scene Reliability Prior estimated from the global driving context, SARFusion further incorporates object-level evidence to route each query to the most suitable branch. This query-wise routing strategy alleviates harmful cross-modal interference while preserving the benefits of multimodal fusion when complementary cues are trustworthy. On the nuScenes test set, SARFusion achieves strong performance with 72.5 mAP and 74.4 NDS. Extensive analyses demonstrate its robustness under challenging conditions, including sensor corruptions and environmental changes.

</details>


### [42] [TOLA: Text-aware One-Step Latent Adaptation for Diffusion-based Text Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29240)
*Yike Xu,Yue Shi,Yong Guo,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TOLA，一种文本感知的一步式隐空间适配框架，用于文本图像超分辨率（TSR）。它通过置信度加权的文本条件模块和轻量隐空间残差校正模块，避免了迭代式图像-文本扩散，在抑制错误 OCR 先验的同时修复笔画细节，并在多个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的 TSR 方法依赖多步预测高分辨率图像或其文本先验，计算成本高、推理延迟大；更严重的是，错误的文本先验会在去噪过程中反复注入，使图像与文本预测相互强化，把早期识别错误逐步放大为清晰但语义错误的字符。针对这两大局限，需要一种无需迭代图像-文本扩散、能抑制错误语义先验的框架。

Method: 提出 TOLA（Text-aware One-step Latent Adaptation）框架，包含两个关键模块：1）置信度加权的文本条件模块，仅构建一次语义条件，并在污染图像重建前抑制不可靠的 OCR 预测；2）轻量级隐空间残差校正模块，显式估计并校正结构化残差误差，以恢复缺失或失真的笔画细节。整个流程为一步式隐空间适配，不进行迭代扩散。

Result: 在 CTR-TSR-Test（×4）和 RealCE-200 两个基准上，TOLA 在全部评价指标上均取得当前最优（SOTA）性能；尤其在 CTR-TSR-Test 上，TOLA 在 PSNR 指标上比现有扩散式 TSR 方法至少高出 2.72 dB。

Conclusion: TOLA 通过一次性语义条件构建与结构化残差校正，既消除了多步扩散带来的高成本和错误先验放大问题，又显著提升了文本图像超分辨率的保真度与可读性，在公开基准上全面超越现有方法，验证了其有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Text image super-resolution (TSR) aims to recover visually faithful and readable text under unknown degradations. Existing diffusion-based methods typically rely on multi-step prediction of either the high-resolution image or its text prior, resulting in prohibitive computational cost and inference latency. More critically, an erroneous text prior may be repeatedly injected into the denoising process, causing image and text predictions to reinforce each other and progressively amplify an early recognition error into a sharp yet semantically incorrect character. To address these limitations, we propose TOLA, a Text-aware One-step Latent Adaptation framework without iterative image-text diffusion. TOLA consists of two key modules. First, a confidence-weighted text conditioning module constructs the semantic condition only once and suppresses unreliable OCR predictions before they contaminate image reconstruction. Second, a lightweight latent residual correction module explicitly estimates and corrects the structured residual errors to recover missing or distorted stroke details. Extensive experiments demonstrate our state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics on both CTR-TSR-Test ($\times 4$) and RealCE-200 benchmarks. It is worth noting that our TOLA consistently surpasses existing diffusion-based TSR methods by at least 2.72 dB in PSNR on CTR-TSR-Test.

</details>


### [43] [IronViT: Toward Efficient Generalist Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29252)
*Jiaxi Huang,Yueqi Hu,Xin Zhu,Xiaopeng Zhang,Huiting Qiao,Yanglin Zhang,Zefeng Ji,Rongxue Li,Yifei Xu,Huiying Yu,Wei Liu,Jiayin Zheng,Yinggan Xu,Peipeng Chen,Yin Zhang,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IronViT 提出"先整合能力、再压缩计算"的蒸馏原则：先将多个专家教师蒸馏到一个 softmax 注意力"能力桥"中，再逐步迁移到混合 softmax-线性注意力编码器，从而在不继承高分辨率 softmax 注意力高昂成本的前提下获得通用视觉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉编码器需要在统一表示中同时捕捉语义、空间、语言对齐和动作相关线索，但当前最强视觉骨干依赖的 softmax 注意力在高分辨率下计算成本过高。直接向高效架构蒸馏多个专家教师会导致表示质量下降，因为学生必须同时调和异构能力并适配不同的 token 混合架构。

Method: 提出 IronViT，核心原则是"先整合能力再约束计算"：第一步将互补的专家教师蒸馏到一个 softmax 注意力能力桥（capability bridge）中，以整合异构能力；第二步将整合后的表示逐步迁移到混合 softmax-线性注意力编码器。同时设计专用数据管线，对蒸馏语料进行筛选以提高信息密度和领域覆盖。

Result: 在识别、检索、密集预测、多模态理解和机器人学习等任务上，IronViT 与领先的专家及通用视觉编码器相当。softmax 能力桥在多模态理解和机器人学习上取得评估骨干中最强的综合表现；混合编码器则保持广泛的迁移性能，且效率优势随输入分辨率提高而增大。

Conclusion: 先整合能力再进行架构转换，可以在不继承传统 softmax 注意力高分辨率高成本的前提下，得到通用视觉编码器，证明了该蒸馏策略的有效性。

Abstract: A generalist vision encoder must capture semantic, spatial, language-aligned, and action-relevant cues within a unified representation, yet softmax attention underlying today's most capable visual backbones becomes prohibitively expensive at high resolution. A natural attempt to address both challenges is to distill multiple specialist teachers directly into an efficient architecture. We find that directly coupling these objectives degrades representation quality, as the student must simultaneously reconcile heterogeneous capabilities and adapt them to a different token-mixing architecture. We introduce IronViT, built on a simple principle: consolidate capabilities before constraining computation. IronViT first distills complementary specialists into a softmax attention capability bridge, then progressively transfers the consolidated representation to a hybrid softmax-linear attention encoder. A purpose-built data pipeline further curates the distillation corpus for higher information density and broader domain coverage. Across recognition, retrieval, dense prediction, multimodal understanding, and robotic learning, IronViT is competitive with leading specialist and generalist vision encoders. The softmax bridge achieves the strongest aggregate performance in multimodal understanding and robotic learning among the evaluated backbones, while the hybrid encoder retains broad transfer performance with an efficiency advantage that grows with input resolution. Together, these results show that consolidating capabilities before architectural conversion can yield a generalist visual encoder without inheriting the prohibitive high-resolution cost of conventional softmax attention.

</details>


### [44] [Deep learning of longitudinal visual fields predicts glaucoma progression rate and identifies fast progressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29256)
*Taiabur Rahman,Siddiqur Rahman,Muhammad Moniruzzaman,Ummay Kawsar,Sayedatunnessa Ratna,Shadman Siddique,Rafsan Siddique,Tausif Ahmad,Tahsin Ahmad,Golam Rabbani*

TL;DR: 本文提出GLAM（青光眼纵向分析模型），一种基于深度学习的框架，可仅利用常规视野计（VF）数据预测青光眼MD和VFI进展速率，并检测快速进展者，性能显著优于传统回归基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼是不可逆失明的首要原因，及时识别快速进展者对预防残疾至关重要。但现有方法通过MD对时间的普通最小二乘回归来估计进展，需要6-10次、跨越数年的视野测试才能获得可靠斜率，效率低下、延迟诊断。

Method: GLAM采用深度学习框架，输入纵向Humphrey 24-2总偏差序列及五项临床特征，利用基于注意力的融合机制和任意（认知类）不确定性建模，预测MD和视野指数（VFI）的进展速率。

Result: 在华盛顿大学Humphrey视野数据集（4,276只患者眼）上，GLAM的MD速率平均绝对误差为0.139 dB/年（R²=0.927，较岭回归基线降低73.5%），快速进展者检测AUC达0.990。

Conclusion: 仅使用视野数据的深度学习即可在进展预测上匹敌多模态流程，表明利用常规采集的视野检查即可实现青光眼进展预后的可靠预测。

Abstract: Glaucoma is the leading cause of irreversible blindness, and timely identification of fast progressors is essential to prevent disability. Current practice estimates progression by ordinary least-squares regression of mean deviation (MD) on time, requiring 6--10 visual field (VF) tests over several years to obtain a reliable slope. We present GLAM (Glaucoma Longitudinal Analysis Model), a deep learning framework that ingests longitudinal Humphrey 24-2 total deviation sequences with five clinical features and predicts MD and visual field index progression rates using attention-based fusion and aleatoric uncertainty. On the open-access University of Washington Humphrey Visual Field dataset (4,276 patient-eyes), GLAM achieved an MD-rate mean absolute error of 0.139 dB yr$^{-1}$ ($R^2 = 0.927$; 73.5% reduction over a ridge baseline) and an AUC of 0.990 for fast-progressor detection. VF-only deep learning can match multimodal pipelines for progression prognostication using routinely collected perimetry alone.

</details>
